Merged in PR #19; **the prerequisite M14 was waiting on.**\n- [x] **AX0 — Objective-driven agent experience (documentation and Agent Skills only).** The Goal-to-Solution lifecycle, the SolutionPlan design, capability-discovery guidance, the mirrored `solve-business-goal` Skill, the canonical full-funnel worked example, 10 JTBD-AX rows and the E2E-G1 benchmark gates. **No runtime code; AX1–AX5 are not implemented.**\n- [x] **M14a — Delivery execution.** The three M13 delivery records evolve to revision 2 and gain a lifecycle: eight human-driven transitions over explicit tables, block evidence with a required reason, and a hierarchy gate that closes a project only over completed work (`docs/plans/milestone-14-delivery-economics-acceptance.md`, ADR-019 addendum 1).\n- [x] **AX1 — Deterministic application inspection (platform).** `accordo app inspect [--json]`: one deterministic, source-only, read-only document describing the composed application — the package graph, the resolved capability graph including unresolved edges, records with their revisions, actions with declared transition metadata, policies and provider definitions, deterministic problems for an invalid composition, and machine-readable limitations (`docs/APPLICATION_INSPECTION.md`). Merged in PR #22. It does **not** aggregate JTBD or quality-gate evidence, and does not open any database.\n- [x] **M14b1 — Delivery economics.** A versioned fingerprinted cost policy, append-only time and expense evidence, an immutable versioned economic plan and a reproducible contribution estimate grouped by currency, plus the `delivery-economics@1` capability (`docs/DELIVERY_ECONOMICS.md`). Merged in PR #23 after an adversarial review that fixed three defects: a recurring obligation set against a spend to date, a silently substituted cost-policy version, and a divergent retry absorbed under a reused key. A contribution estimate now exists **only** where every commercial input in that currency is one-time.\n- [x] **AX2 — Machine-readable Solution Plans (platform).** `solutionPlanContract: 1` with a revision and canonical fingerprint, six decision types over the repository's own hierarchy, six evidence categories with resolving citations, a closed approval vocabulary, and `accordo solution inspect|validate|check` — `check` binds a plan to this project's `app inspect` report and reports `PLAN_STALE` (`docs/SOLUTION_PLAN.md`, ADR-020). Merged in PR #24 after an adversarial review that fixed seven defects, including an open key set on plan objects, a citation graph with no direction, and a composition binding that read the plan rather than the project. It is **not a planner and not a runtime**: nothing writes a plan, nothing executes one, and a plan may not carry a command.\n- [x] **M14b2 — Delivery change requests, deliverables and acceptance.** Merged in PR #25. It's zero-runtime-dependency: `package-lock.json` declares no `dependencies` at all — everything runs on plain Node ≥22.16 (`node packages/cli/bin/accordo.js ...`), which is why there's no `node_modules` and none is needed.\n\nStructure: `packages/` holds ~20 internal packages — a generated-code core (`app`, `modules`, `actions`, `pipelines`, `contracts`, `domains`, `sdk`) plus optional domain packages (`intelligence`, `commercial`, `signature`, `contracts`/activation, `delivery`, `service`, `lifecycle`, `work`), a `cli`, an `mcp` server, a `docs-mcp` server, and `create-accordo` (project bootstrapper). `apps/` has an `admin` UI and `server`. Root docs (`AGENTS.md`, `PRODUCT.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, `DECISIONS.md` with 31 ADRs, `docs/PROJECT_STATUS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `TASKS.md`) are extensive and explicitly kept as the single sources of truth for status/decisions/next-work — this is not just source code, it's a heavily process-governed repo (there's even an MCP config wiring the CLI itself as a stdio MCP server).\n\n## State (per `docs/PROJECT_STATUS.md`, generated 2026-08-12)\n\n- **Domain chain shipped end-to-end**: lead capture → intelligence/scoring/routing → pipeline → quotes/catalog → signature → immutable Order → contract activation/subscriptions → delivery handover/execution/economics/change/acceptance → service operations (coverage, entitlements, SLA cases) → renewal & expansion (records intent only, doesn't yet renew/cancel/price/schedule anything).\n- **Coding-agent DX tooling shipped**: `app inspect`, Solution Plans, Project Doctor, Package Scaffold, Package Conformance, Project Verify, Scenario Evidence, Legacy Characterization.
